# Skills · S · part 178

874 entries, ssak-orchestrate to st-browser-evidence. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ssak-orchestrate**: Open a new Claude Code pane in Herdr and hand it a task, so the main agent stays free and the user can watch or steer the new session. Also lists, reads and prompts the panes it started. Only inside a Herdr pane (HERDR_ENV=1). Use for "/ssak-orchestrate medium &lt;task&gt;", "/ssak-orchestrate sonnet low &lt;task&gt;", "/ssak-orchestrate list", "/ssak-orchestrate read &lt;name&gt;" or "open a new claude window in herdr and do X". ([Sean-mn/ssak-ssak](https://github.com/Sean-mn/ssak-ssak/tree/HEAD/skills/ssak-orchestrate) · ★4)
- **ssak-respond**: Answer the review comments on your own GitHub pull request. Reads the unresolved review threads, sorts each into fix, answer or decline, fixes the code with local commits after the user approves the plan, then posts a reply in each thread after the user approves the drafts. Never pushes and never resolves a thread. Use for "/ssak-respond 42", "/ssak-respond" on the PR's branch or "address the review comments on PR 42". ([Sean-mn/ssak-ssak](https://github.com/Sean-mn/ssak-ssak/tree/HEAD/skills/ssak-respond) · ★4)
- **ssak-review**: Review one or more GitHub pull requests, focused on what each PR changes and what could hurt production, plus lint and team rules. Effort level sets how deep it goes. Two or more PRs are reviewed in parallel, one reviewer agent per PR, and reported together. Reports findings by severity to the user, or posts them as inline PR comments with --comment. Use for "/ssak-review high 42 --comment", "/ssak-review 42 43" or "review PR 42". ([Sean-mn/ssak-ssak](https://github.com/Sean-mn/ssak-ssak/tree/HEAD/skills/ssak-review) · ★4)
- **ssam**: Use Ssam to initialize and render a Canvas 2D sketch through its sketch manager. This guide covers a static 32×32 pixel fixture only; export and animation APIs remain untested. ([conorluddy/vcr](https://github.com/conorluddy/vcr/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ssam) · 2 copies)
